# NOX HELION · FINAL UNIFIED READY SOURCES INDEX · 2026-10-30

**A consolidated index of all currently saved catalog candidates**, not evidence that all requirements are covered, all sources licensed or compatible, or modules downloaded. This master file directly enumerates every distinct source URL from 10 project catalogs with its source category and originating file. Contains source Google Docs and reference documentation too; **unique links ≠ ready integrations**. Unpinned/unreviewed by default. Separate from product PR #162. Approved identities, HOME/MENU/WORK, existing OneHost/NSIS/Ollama/SQLite/RAG/STOP/ACL preserved.

## Selected product engineering rules
- Choose existing reusable implementation first; three candidates per RequirementID is the **mapping goal**, not proof this current index has completed every requirement. One chosen dependency, backups as alternatives.
- MAIN V3 in Drive index currently returns 404 to this connection; menu count/owners cannot be certified. Drive WEB Release specifies 540 numbered catalog entries across 30 families, Design Layout has S01–S42, MENU tree V2 has 10 *top-level worlds* and 193 entries; do not conflate them. Independent full requirements reconciliation remains pending.
- **AI ENGINE PACK 100** separately lists 100 engines/frameworks, and **MODEL WEIGHTS 38** separately lists 38 model cards with hardware gates. HunyuanVideo 1.5 12 GB Windows HOLD per official model card (Linux, ≥14GB VRAM offload).
- Need per-candidate source commit/version, Windows/Web/runtime check, code/model license, download checksum, security review and native acceptance before integration.
